        $(document).ready(function () {
            $('#alertForm').submit(function () {
                var that = $(this)
                var formData = $(this).serialize();
                var url = 'https://www.propertyturkey.com/real_estate/save_alert';
                $.post(url, formData).done(function (data) {
                    var message = data.message;
                    if (data.status === 1) {
                        $('.message').text(message).addClass('success').removeClass('closed')
                        setTimeout(function () {
                            $('.message').addClass('closed').removeClass('success')
                            $.fancybox.close( true );
                        }, 2000)
                    }else{
                        $('.message').text(message).addClass('error').removeClass('closed')
                        setTimeout(function () {
                            $('.message').addClass('closed').removeClass('error')
                        }, 2000)
                    }
                });
                return false
            });
        })
